const SOURCE_LEGACY_REQUEST = '.cache/dmi-legacy-current-original-requests.json';
const SOURCE_LEGACY_OUTPUT = '.cache/dmi-legacy-current-originals.json';
const SOURCE_DEPLOYED_COPY = '.cache/deployed-dmi-bulk-cache.json';
const SOURCE_LEGACY_INPUTS = Object.freeze({ zones: 'data/zones.geojson',
  deployedBulk: SOURCE_DEPLOYED_COPY, forecast: 'data/live/dmi-forecast-cache.json',
  candidate: '.cache/dmi-candidate-progress.json', active: '.cache/dmi-active-complete.json' });
const SOURCE_LEGACY_BINDING_KEYS = Object.freeze(Object.keys(ravScoreModelBinding()));
function sourceLegacyExpectationMetadata(plan, requestsSha256, target) {
  const sha = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^sha256:[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(value);
  const version2 = isPlainObject(plan) && plan.schemaVersion === 2;
  const fields = 'documentContexts,historyDemandSha256,kind,modelBinding,productionReferenceAt,projectionWitnesses,reconstructionRequests,registrySha256,schemaVersion,sourceHashes,sourcePaths';
  const exactFields = version2 ? 'documentContexts,existingNativeOriginals,' + fields.slice('documentContexts,'.length) : fields;
  if (!isPlainObject(plan) || ![1, 2].includes(plan.schemaVersion)
    || plan.kind !== 'DMI_LEGACY_CURRENT_ORIGINAL_RECONSTRUCTION_REQUESTS'
    || Object.keys(plan).sort().join(',') !== exactFields
    || plan.productionReferenceAt !== sourceNormalUtcHour(target) || !sha(requestsSha256)
    || !sha(plan.historyDemandSha256) || !sha(plan.registrySha256)
    || !isPlainObject(plan.sourceHashes) || !same(plan.sourcePaths, SOURCE_LEGACY_INPUTS)
    || Object.keys(plan.sourceHashes).sort().join(',') !== 'active,candidate,deployedBulk,forecast,zones'
    || Object.entries(plan.sourceHashes).some(([role, value]) =>
      !sha(value) && !(value === null && ['candidate', 'active'].includes(role)))
    || !Array.isArray(plan.reconstructionRequests) || plan.reconstructionRequests.length > 2 * 673 * 124
    || version2 && (!Array.isArray(plan.existingNativeOriginals) || !plan.existingNativeOriginals.length
      || plan.reconstructionRequests.length + plan.existingNativeOriginals.length > 2 * 673 * 124)
    || !Array.isArray(plan.projectionWitnesses) || plan.projectionWitnesses.length > 673 * 124
    || !Array.isArray(plan.documentContexts) || plan.documentContexts.length > 3) sourceOriginalFailure('LEGACY_REQUEST_METADATA_INVALID');
  assertRavScoreModelBinding(plan.modelBinding, 'SOURCE legacy current issuer binding');
  // Bound ENCODED strings, not ASCII/code-point counts: JSON escaping can grow.
  // The exact issuer fixes all eleven values; its current maximum encoded
  // string is 98 bytes. This deliberately separate metadata bound also admits
  // every nine-string combination <=256 encoded bytes, plus the two hex64s.
  if (SOURCE_LEGACY_BINDING_KEYS.some(key => typeof plan.modelBinding[key] !== 'string'
    || Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(plan.modelBinding[key]), 'utf8') > 256)) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('LEGACY_BINDING_METADATA_BOUND');
  }
  const expectation = { productionReferenceAt: plan.productionReferenceAt,
    modelBinding: plan.modelBinding, historyDemandSha256: plan.historyDemandSha256,
    registrySha256: plan.registrySha256, sourceHashes: plan.sourceHashes, requestsSha256 };
  const serialized = JSON.stringify(expectation);
  // Symbolic compact maximum =3435 bytes: nine encoded strings256, two
  // hex64 strings66, fixed keys, a UTC24 and eight SHA71s. Null is shorter.
  if (Buffer.byteLength(serialized, 'utf8') > 4096) sourceOriginalFailure('LEGACY_EXPECTATION_METADATA_BOUND');
  return freezeSourceOriginalMetadata({ expectation, serialized,
    requestCount: plan.reconstructionRequests.length });
}
export async function sourceNormalDeployedCopyInput(holder) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder), copy = owner.normalDeployedCopy;
  if (!copy || copy.completed || owner.activeOperation !== copy.operation
    || !owner.workingOperation?.completed || copy.input.holder !== holder) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_COPY_NOT_OWNED');
  }
  await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, copy.input.original);
  return copy.input;
}
export async function assertSourceNormalDeployedCopyInput(holder, input) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder), copy = owner.normalDeployedCopy;
  if (!copy || copy.completed || copy.input !== input || owner.activeOperation !== copy.operation) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_COPY_NOT_OWNED');
  }
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, input.original);
  for (const directory of input.directories) {
    const now = await fs.lstat(directory.path, { bigint: true });
    if (!now.isDirectory() || now.isSymbolicLink()
      || !sameSourceOriginalStat(directory.stat, now, false)) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_DIRECTORY_CHANGED');
  }
  sourceOriginalOwner(holder); // includes the same issued absolute deadline
}
async function runSourceNormalDeployedBulkCopy(holder) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  if (owner.activeOperation || owner.normalDeployedCopy || !owner.workingOperation?.completed) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_COPY_REUSED');
  }
  const operation = Object.freeze({});
  const copy = { operation, input: null, completed: false, row: null };
  owner.normalDeployedCopy = copy; owner.activeOperation = operation;
  try {
    await assertSourceOriginalProgress(holder);
    const source = currentSourceWorkingRows(owner).get(path.join(owner.context.repository, 'data/live/dmi-bulk-cache.json'));
    if (!source) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_SOURCE_REQUIRED');
    await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, source);
    const located = await locateSourceNormalOutput(owner, { name: 'deployedBulkCopy' }, SOURCE_DEPLOYED_COPY);
    if (!located.absent) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_DESTINATION_EXISTS');
    copy.input = Object.freeze({ holder, root: owner.context.repository, original: source,
      handle: source.handle, bytes: source.bytes, sha256: source.sha256,
      destination: located.path, directories: Object.freeze(located.directories) });
    const progress = await import('./weather-component-progress-cache.mjs');
    await progress.copySourceNormalDeployedBulk(holder);
    const result = progress.sourceNormalDeployedCopyCompletion(holder);
    if (result.input !== copy.input || !result.closed || result.bytes !== source.bytes
      || result.sha256 !== source.sha256) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_COPY_UNPROVED');
    const row = await openSourceOutputRow(owner, owner.context.repository, located.path, source);
    copy.row = row;
    await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, source);
    await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
    await assertSourceOriginalProgress(holder);
    sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
    if (owner.activeOperation !== operation) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_DEPLOYED_COPY_CHANGED');
    copy.completed = true; owner.activeOperation = null;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
async function runSourceNormalLegacyCurrentRequests(holder, operation) {
  const state = sourceDmiWorkingState(holder, operation), issued = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  const prelude = issued.normalPrelude;
  if (state.dmiStarted || prelude.legacyCurrentRequests || !prelude.generationPreparation
    || prelude.handoff?.reused || !state.owner.normalDeployedCopy?.completed) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_REQUEST_PHASE_REUSED');
  }
  for (const relative of [SOURCE_LEGACY_REQUEST, SOURCE_LEGACY_OUTPUT]) {
    const stat = await fs.lstat(path.join(state.owner.context.repository, relative)).catch(error => {
      if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error;
    });
    if (stat !== null) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_UNOWNED_FILE');
  }
  if (!issued.normalRoute.request.checkpointOnly || prelude.currentHistoryPlan?.required !== true) {
    prelude.legacyCurrentRequests = Object.freeze({ required: false });
    return;
  }
  const phase = await runSourceRequiredProgram(holder, operation, 'legacyCurrentRequests');
  const generation = await assertSourceNormalGeneration(holder, operation);
  const row = generation.rows.find(item => item.path === path.join(state.owner.context.repository, SOURCE_LEGACY_REQUEST));
  if (!row || !phase.outputRows.includes(row) || !phase.completed || !phase.transportClosed
    || phase.result.exitCode !== 0 || phase.result.outputs.requests_sha256 !== 'sha256:' + row.sha256) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_REQUEST_PHASE_UNPROVED');
  }
  const plan = JSON.parse((await sourceNormalRowBytes(holder, row, 192 * 1024 * 1024)).toString('utf8'));
  const metadata = sourceLegacyExpectationMetadata(plan, 'sha256:' + row.sha256, state.lockedTarget);
  const inputs = new Map(), expectation = metadata.expectation;
  for (const [role, relative] of Object.entries(SOURCE_LEGACY_INPUTS)) {
    const file = path.join(state.owner.context.repository, relative);
    const stat = await fs.lstat(file, { bigint: true }).catch(error => {
      if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error;
    });
    if (stat === null) {
      if (!['candidate', 'active'].includes(role) || expectation.sourceHashes[role] !== null) {
        sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_INPUT_MISSING');
      }
      inputs.set(role, null); continue;
    }
    const input = generation.rows.find(item => item.path === file)
      ?? await openSourceOutputRow(state.owner, state.owner.context.repository, file);
    await hashSourceOriginalHandle(state.owner, input);
    if (expectation.sourceHashes[role] !== 'sha256:' + input.sha256) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_INPUT_CHANGED');
    inputs.set(role, input);
  }
  const registry = await openSourceOutputRow(state.owner, state.owner.context.repository,
    path.join(state.owner.context.repository, 'data/live/coastal-parts-v2.json'));
  if (expectation.registrySha256 !== 'sha256:' + registry.sha256
    || expectation.historyDemandSha256 !== 'sha256:' + prelude.currentHistoryPlan.row.sha256
    || inputs.get('deployedBulk')?.sha256 !== state.owner.normalDeployedCopy.row.sha256) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_INPUT_CHANGED');
  }
  inputs.set('registry', registry);
  inputs.set('history', prelude.currentHistoryPlan.row);
  await assertSourceNormalGeneration(holder, operation);
  sourceDmiWorkingState(holder, operation);
  prelude.legacyCurrentRequests = Object.freeze({ required: true, phase, row,
    requestsSha256: 'sha256:' + row.sha256, expectation: metadata.expectation,
    expectationJson: metadata.serialized, requestCount: metadata.requestCount, inputs });
}
async function assertSourceNormalLegacyCurrentRequests(holder, operation, beforeDmi) {
  const state = sourceDmiWorkingState(holder, operation), prelude = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder).normalPrelude;
  const selected = prelude.legacyCurrentRequests;
  if (!selected || typeof selected.required !== 'boolean') sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_REQUEST_REQUIRED');
  if (!selected.required) {
    for (const relative of [SOURCE_LEGACY_REQUEST, SOURCE_LEGACY_OUTPUT]) {
      if (await fs.lstat(path.join(state.owner.context.repository, relative)).catch(error => {
        if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error;
      })) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_UNOWNED_FILE');
    }
    return;
  }
  await hashSourceOriginalHandle(state.owner, selected.row);
  if ('sha256:' + selected.row.sha256 !== selected.requestsSha256
    || !selected.phase.completed || !selected.phase.transportClosed) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_REQUEST_CHANGED');
  for (const [role, original] of selected.inputs) {
    if (!beforeDmi && ['candidate', 'active'].includes(role)) continue;
    if (original) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(state.owner, original);
    else if (await fs.lstat(path.join(state.owner.context.repository, SOURCE_LEGACY_INPUTS[role])).catch(error => {
      if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error;
    })) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_ABSENCE_CHANGED');
  }
  if (!beforeDmi) {
    const output = await fs.lstat(path.join(state.owner.context.repository, SOURCE_LEGACY_OUTPUT), { bigint: true }).catch(error => {
      if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error;
    });
    const generation = await assertSourceNormalGeneration(holder, operation);
    const row = generation.rows.find(item => item.path === path.join(state.owner.context.repository, SOURCE_LEGACY_OUTPUT));
    if (selected.requestCount > 0 ? !output || !row || !state.outputRows.includes(row) : output !== null) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_LEGACY_OUTPUT_UNPROVED');
    }
    if (row) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(state.owner, row);
  }
  sourceDmiWorkingState(holder, operation);
}

const SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_STAGES = new Set(['UNKNOWN','SOURCE_GUARD','ROUTE','PRELUDE','PREFLIGHT','ADMIN','HANDOFF','ORIGINAL_SELECTION','CHECKPOINT_PROTECTED','ARCHIVED_READERS','PRIVATE_ROOT','PROGRESS_CACHE','ORIGINAL_INSTALL','POST_INSTALL','PROGRESS_BASE','PROGRESS_UNION','PRODUCER_CHAIN']);
const SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_NODE_CODES = new Set(['ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND','MODULE_NOT_FOUND','ERR_REQUIRE_ASYNC_MODULE','ERR_REQUIRE_ESM','ERR_UNKNOWN_FILE_EXTENSION','ERR_UNSUPPORTED_DIR_IMPORT','ERR_INVALID_ARG_TYPE','ERR_INVALID_ARG_VALUE','ERR_INVALID_URL']);
const SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_PRIVATE_CODES = new Set(['PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_INELIGIBLE','PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_EXPIRED','PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_REMOTE_ERROR','PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_NO_COMPATIBLE_GENERATION']);
const SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_REJECTIONS = new Set(['NONE','UNKNOWN','EXPIRED','FILESYSTEM_CAPACITY','FILESYSTEM_PERMISSION','FILESYSTEM_READ_ONLY','FILESYSTEM_RESOURCE_LIMIT','STORAGE_OR_OBJECT_INTEGRITY','ARCHIVE_OR_PAYLOAD_INTEGRITY','MODEL_BINDING','CONTRACT_HASHES','DATASET_IDENTITY','TIME_BOUNDS','DESCRIPTOR_BUNDLE_MISMATCH','BUNDLE_STRUCTURE_OR_CONTENT','MODEL_OR_CONTRACT_INELIGIBLE','ARCHIVE_ENVELOPE_DECODE','ARCHIVE_STAGE_CREATE','ARCHIVE_FILE_PATH','ARCHIVE_FILE_PARENT','ARCHIVE_FILE_WRITE','ARCHIVE_STAGE_COMMIT']);
const sourceGuardDiagnosticReasons = new WeakMap();
const sourceDriverDiagnosticStages = new WeakMap();
const sourceDriverDiagnosticFailures = new WeakMap();
const sourceUnknownDiagnostic = Object.freeze({stage:'UNKNOWN',reason:'UNKNOWN',nodeClass:'UNKNOWN',rejection:'UNKNOWN'});
function sourceGuardError(code) {
  const error = new Error(code);
  // Only existing fixed literal codes are observable. Unknown strings are not.
  if (SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_GUARD_CODES.has(code)) sourceGuardDiagnosticReasons.set(error, code);
  return error;
}
function sourceDiagnosticOwnString(error, key) {
  const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(error, key);
  return descriptor && Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value') && typeof descriptor.value === 'string'
    ? descriptor.value : null;
}
function rememberSourceDriverFailure(driver, error) {
  // Never replace the first failure if observation itself is unavailable.
  try {
    const stage = sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.get(driver);
    if (!SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_STAGES.has(stage) || error === null || typeof error !== 'object'
      || sourcePreludeTypes.isProxy(error) || !sourcePreludeTypes.isNativeError(error)
      || sourceDriverDiagnosticFailures.has(error)) return;
    for (const key of ['code','message','rejectionCodes']) {
      const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(error, key);
      if (descriptor && !Object.hasOwn(descriptor, 'value')) return;
    }
    const ownCode = sourceDiagnosticOwnString(error, 'code');
    const ownMessage = sourceDiagnosticOwnString(error, 'message');
    const reason = sourceGuardDiagnosticReasons.get(error)
      ?? (SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_GUARD_CODES.has(ownMessage) ? ownMessage
        : SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_NODE_CODES.has(ownCode) || SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_PRIVATE_CODES.has(ownCode)
          ? ownCode : 'UNKNOWN');
    const prototypes = [[Error.prototype,'ERROR'],[TypeError.prototype,'TYPE_ERROR'],
      [ReferenceError.prototype,'REFERENCE_ERROR'],[SyntaxError.prototype,'SYNTAX_ERROR'],
      [RangeError.prototype,'RANGE_ERROR'],[AggregateError.prototype,'AGGREGATE_ERROR']];
    const nodeClass = prototypes.find(([prototype]) => Object.getPrototypeOf(error) === prototype)?.[1] ?? 'UNKNOWN';
    let rejection = 'NONE';
    const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(error, 'rejectionCodes');
    if (descriptor) {
      rejection = 'UNKNOWN';
      const codes = descriptor.value;
      if (Array.isArray(codes) && !sourcePreludeTypes.isProxy(codes)) {
        const length = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(codes, 'length');
        const first = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(codes, '0');
        if (length && Object.hasOwn(length,'value') && Number.isSafeInteger(length.value)
          && length.value > 0 && length.value <= 8 && first && Object.hasOwn(first,'value')
          && SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_REJECTIONS.has(first.value)) rejection = first.value;
      }
    }
    sourceDriverDiagnosticFailures.set(error,Object.freeze({stage,reason,nodeClass,rejection}));
  } catch {}
}
export function sourceFailureDiagnostic(error) {
  // Read-only observation of the actual private error identity; not an issuer.
  if (error === null || typeof error !== 'object' || sourcePreludeTypes.isProxy(error)) return sourceUnknownDiagnostic;
  const recorded = sourceDriverDiagnosticFailures.get(error);
  if (recorded) return recorded;
  const reason = sourceGuardDiagnosticReasons.get(error);
  return reason ? Object.freeze({stage:'SOURCE_GUARD',reason,nodeClass:'ERROR',rejection:'NONE'}) : sourceUnknownDiagnostic;
}
// SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_END

const sourceDeadlineFailure = code => { throw sourceGuardError(code); };
function captureSourceDeadlineProcess() {
  const identity = Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(sourceJobEnvironmentKeys.map(key => [key, process.env[key]])));
  const positive = value => typeof value === 'string' && /^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(value)
    && Number.isSafeInteger(Number(value));
  if (identity.GITHUB_ACTIONS !== 'true' || identity.GITHUB_REF !== 'refs/heads/main'
    || identity.RUNNER_OS !== 'Linux' || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+\/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$/.test(identity.GITHUB_REPOSITORY ?? '')
    || !positive(identity.GITHUB_RUN_ID) || !positive(identity.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT)
    || !/^[a-f0-9]{40}$/.test(identity.GITHUB_SHA ?? '')
    || typeof identity.RUNNER_NAME !== 'string' || !identity.RUNNER_NAME.trim()
    || typeof identity.RUNNER_TRACKING_ID !== 'string' || !identity.RUNNER_TRACKING_ID.trim()
    || !['true', 'false'].includes(identity.RAVRADAR_SOURCE_EXTENDED_PROVIDER_BOOTSTRAP)) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_PROCESS_IDENTITY_INVALID');
  }
  assertSourceDeadlineProcess(identity);
  return identity;
}
function assertSourceDeadlineProcess(identity) {
  if (sourceJobEnvironmentKeys.some(key => process.env[key] !== identity[key])
    || execFileSync('git', ['rev-parse', 'HEAD^{commit}'], {
      cwd: PRIVATE_RUNTIME_REPOSITORY_ROOT, encoding: 'utf8',
    }).trim() !== identity.GITHUB_SHA) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_PROCESS_IDENTITY_CHANGED');
}
function sourceClockSample() {
  const monoBefore = performance.now();
  const wall = Date.now();
  const monoAfter = performance.now();
  return { monoBefore, wall, monoAfter };
}
function intersectSourceClock(clock, sample) {
  if (!sample || !Number.isFinite(sample.monoBefore) || !Number.isFinite(sample.monoAfter)
    || !Number.isSafeInteger(sample.wall) || sample.monoBefore < 0
    || sample.monoBefore > sample.monoAfter || sample.monoBefore < clock.lastMono
    || sample.wall < clock.lastWall) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_CLOCK_INVALID');
  const lower = Math.max(clock.lower, sample.wall - sample.monoAfter);
  const upper = Math.min(clock.upper, sample.wall + 1 - sample.monoBefore);
  if (!(lower < upper)) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_CLOCK_INCONSISTENT');
  clock.lower = lower; clock.upper = upper;
  clock.lastMono = sample.monoAfter; clock.lastWall = sample.wall;
}
function acceptSourceMetadataTiming(clock, response) {
  intersectSourceClock(clock, response.before);
  intersectSourceClock(clock, response.after);
  if (response.after.monoAfter - response.before.monoBefore > 15000) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_METADATA_BODY_DEADLINE');
  }
  const server = typeof response.serverDate === 'string' ? Date.parse(response.serverDate) : NaN;
  if (!Number.isFinite(server) || new Date(server).toUTCString() !== response.serverDate
    || server > response.after.wall || server + 1000 <= response.before.wall) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_HTTP_DATE_INVALID');
  }
}
function assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder) {
  const state = sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(holder);
  if (!state) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_DEADLINE_NOT_ISSUED');
  assertSourceDeadlineProcess(state.identity);
  intersectSourceClock(state.clock, sourceClockSample());
  // Never widen an issued deadline when later clock evidence narrows an interval.
  state.deadline = Math.min(state.deadline, state.endEpoch - state.clock.upper);
  if (!(state.deadline > state.clock.lastMono)) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_DEADLINE_EXPIRED');
  return state;
}
async function issuePrivateSourceJobDeadline(identity, metadata) {
  const clock = { lower: -Infinity, upper: Infinity, lastMono: -Infinity, lastWall: -Infinity };
  const root = '/repos/' + identity.GITHUB_REPOSITORY + '/actions/runs/' + identity.GITHUB_RUN_ID
    + '/attempts/' + identity.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT;
  const runResult = await metadata.readJsonWithTiming(root);
  acceptSourceMetadataTiming(clock, runResult);
  const run = runResult.json;
  const workflow = '.github/workflows/update-and-deploy.yml';
  if (!run || run.id !== Number(identity.GITHUB_RUN_ID)
    || run.run_attempt !== Number(identity.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT) || run.head_sha !== identity.GITHUB_SHA
    || run.head_branch !== 'main' || run.path !== workflow || run.status !== 'in_progress'
    || run.conclusion !== null || !['schedule', 'workflow_dispatch'].includes(run.event)
    || run.repository?.full_name !== identity.GITHUB_REPOSITORY
    || run.head_repository?.full_name !== identity.GITHUB_REPOSITORY) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_RUN_IDENTITY_INVALID');
  }
  const jobsResult = await metadata.readJsonWithTiming(root + '/jobs?per_page=100');
  acceptSourceMetadataTiming(clock, jobsResult);
  const list = jobsResult.json;
  if (jobsResult.hasNextPage || !list || !Array.isArray(list.jobs)
    || !Number.isSafeInteger(list.total_count) || list.total_count < 1 || list.total_count > 100
    || list.jobs.length !== list.total_count
    || list.jobs.some(job => !Number.isSafeInteger(job.id) || job.id < 1)
    || new Set(list.jobs.map(job => job.id)).size !== list.jobs.length) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_LIST_INCOMPLETE');
  }
  const name = SOURCE_GATE_PRODUCERS[workflow].gates[0].job;
  const jobs = list.jobs.filter(job => job.name === name);
  if (jobs.length !== 1) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_IDENTITY_INVALID');
  const job = jobs[0];
  if (job.run_id !== run.id || job.head_sha !== identity.GITHUB_SHA
    || (Object.hasOwn(job, 'run_attempt') && job.run_attempt !== run.run_attempt)
    || job.status !== 'in_progress' || job.conclusion !== null || job.completed_at !== null
    || !Number.isSafeInteger(job.runner_id) || job.runner_id < 1 || job.runner_name !== identity.RUNNER_NAME) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_IDENTITY_INVALID');
  }
  const started = typeof job.started_at === 'string' ? Date.parse(job.started_at) : NaN;
  const canonicalStarted = typeof job.started_at === 'string'
    ? job.started_at.replace(/(?<!\.\d{3})Z$/, '.000Z') : '';
  if (!Number.isFinite(started) || new Date(started).toISOString() !== canonicalStarted
    || started > runResult.before.wall) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_STARTED_AT_INVALID');
  const minutes = identity.RAVRADAR_SOURCE_EXTENDED_PROVIDER_BOOTSTRAP === 'true' ? 240 : 230;
  const endEpoch = started + minutes * 60000;
  const holder = Object.freeze({});
  sourceJobDeadlineBrands.set(holder, { identity, clock, endEpoch, jobId: job.id, event: run.event, metadata,
    deadline: endEpoch - clock.upper });
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  return holder;
}
async function readSourceOperationalRoute(holder) {
  const deadline = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  if (deadline.normalRoute) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_ROUTE_ALREADY_SELECTED');
  const event = deadline.event;
  if (!['schedule', 'workflow_dispatch'].includes(event)) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_EVENT_INVALID');
  const keys = ['INPUT_PRODUCE_WEATHER_HANDOFF', 'INPUT_PRODUCE_WEATHER_HANDOFF_CONFIRMATION',
    'INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_WEATHER_HANDOFF_RUN_ID', 'INPUT_RAVSCORE_CANDIDATE_G_ROLLBACK_MODE',
    'INPUT_RAVSCORE_CANDIDATE_G_ROLLBACK_CONFIRMATION', 'INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_FIRST_CUTOVER',
    'INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_FIRST_CUTOVER_CONFIRMATION', 'INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_RETURN',
    'INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_RETURN_CONFIRMATION', 'INPUT_QUICK_CONFIRMATION', 'INPUT_QUICK_PROGRESS_SOURCE',
    'INPUT_CHECKPOINT_ONLY', 'INPUT_CHECKPOINT_TARGET_HOUR'];
  const captured = Object.freeze(Object.fromEntries(keys.map(key => [key, process.env[key]])));
  const flag = key => {
    const value = captured[key] ?? 'false';
    if (value !== 'true' && value !== 'false') sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_REQUEST_INVALID');
    return value === 'true';
  };
  // These are explicit normal request choices, never completed step results.
  const request = Object.freeze({
    produceHandoff: flag('INPUT_PRODUCE_WEATHER_HANDOFF'),
    produceHandoffConfirmation: captured.INPUT_PRODUCE_WEATHER_HANDOFF_CONFIRMATION ?? '',
    handoffRunId: captured.INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_WEATHER_HANDOFF_RUN_ID ?? '',
    rollbackMode: event === 'workflow_dispatch'
      ? captured.INPUT_RAVSCORE_CANDIDATE_G_ROLLBACK_MODE ?? 'none' : 'none',
    rollbackConfirmation: event === 'workflow_dispatch'
      ? captured.INPUT_RAVSCORE_CANDIDATE_G_ROLLBACK_CONFIRMATION ?? '' : '',
    firstCutover: event === 'workflow_dispatch' && flag('INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_FIRST_CUTOVER'),
    firstCutoverConfirmation: event === 'workflow_dispatch'
      ? captured.INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_FIRST_CUTOVER_CONFIRMATION ?? '' : '',
    returnRequested: event === 'workflow_dispatch' && flag('INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_RETURN'),
    returnConfirmation: event === 'workflow_dispatch'
      ? captured.INPUT_RAVSCORE_INTEGRATED_RETURN_CONFIRMATION ?? '' : '',
    quick: flag('INPUT_QUICK_CONFIRMATION'),
    quickProgressSource: captured.INPUT_QUICK_PROGRESS_SOURCE ?? '',
    checkpointOnly: flag('INPUT_CHECKPOINT_ONLY'),
    checkpointTargetHour: captured.INPUT_CHECKPOINT_TARGET_HOUR ?? '',
    extended: deadline.identity.RAVRADAR_SOURCE_EXTENDED_PROVIDER_BOOTSTRAP === 'true',
  });
  if ((request.quick || request.quickProgressSource !== '')
    && !(request.quick && request.quickProgressSource === 'protected-current')
    && (!/^[0-9]+-[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(request.quickProgressSource)
      || /[\r\n]/.test(request.quickProgressSource))) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_QUICK_PROGRESS_SOURCE_INVALID');
  if (request.checkpointOnly) {
    const target = sourceNormalUtcHour(request.checkpointTargetHour);
    if (event !== 'workflow_dispatch' || request.quick || request.extended
      || !/^[1-9][0-9]*-[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(request.quickProgressSource)
      || target === null || target !== sourceNormalUtcHour(process.env.RAVRADAR_PRODUCTION_TARGET_HOUR)) {
      sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_CHECKPOINT_REQUEST_INVALID');
    }
  } else if (request.checkpointTargetHour !== '') sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_CHECKPOINT_REQUEST_INVALID');
  const { RAVSCORE_OPERATIONAL_ACTIVATION_DOCUMENT_KEY: operationalKey,
    RAVSCORE_PROFILE_SELECTION_DOCUMENT_KEY: profileKey,
    resolveOperationalRavScoreModel, operationalResolvedBindingCurrent }
    = await import('./ravscore-operational-activation.mjs');
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  const url = process.env.SUPABASE_URL?.replace(/\/$/, '');
  const key = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  if (!url || !key) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_CENTRAL_CREDENTIALS_REQUIRED');
  const read = createSupabaseAdminRequester({ endpoint: `${url}/rest/v1/admin_documents`, key });
  // Same fixed atomic row query and strict shape as the normal operational read
  // CLI. The normal whole resolver validates the real row/profile/closure pair.
  const rows = await read(`?select=document_key,payload,version&document_key=in.(${operationalKey},${profileKey})`,
    {}, 'atomisk RavScore operation/profil-læsning');
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length > 2) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_CENTRAL_ROWS_INVALID');
  const byKey = new Map();
  for (const row of rows) {
    if (![operationalKey, profileKey].includes(row?.document_key) || byKey.has(row.document_key)
      || !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(row.version)) || Number(row.version) < 1) {
      sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_CENTRAL_ROWS_INVALID');
    }
    byKey.set(row.document_key, row);
  }
  const profileRow = byKey.get(profileKey) ?? null;
  if (profileRow === null) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_CENTRAL_PROFILE_REQUIRED');
  const operationalRow = byKey.get(operationalKey) ?? null;
  const resolved = resolveOperationalRavScoreModel(operationalRow, { profileRow, allowPending: true });
  const bindingCurrent = operationalResolvedBindingCurrent(resolved);
  const requireRoute = condition => {
    if (!condition) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_OPERATIONAL_ACTION_REFUSED');
  };
  let transitions = Number(request.rollbackMode !== 'none') + Number(request.firstCutover)
    + Number(request.returnRequested);
  if (request.produceHandoff) {
    requireRoute(event === 'workflow_dispatch' && deadline.identity.GITHUB_REF === 'refs/heads/main'
      && request.produceHandoffConfirmation === 'PRODUCE-VERIFIED-WEATHER-SOURCE-HANDOFF'
      && resolved.initialCutoverRequired === true
      && ((resolved.model === 'candidate-g' && bindingCurrent === true)
        || (resolved.model === 'legacy-candidate-g' && typeof bindingCurrent === 'boolean')));
    transitions++;
  } else requireRoute(request.produceHandoffConfirmation === '');
  requireRoute(transitions <= 1);
  if (request.firstCutover) requireRoute(event === 'workflow_dispatch'
    && request.firstCutoverConfirmation === 'EXECUTE-INTEGRATED-RAVSCORE-FIRST-CUTOVER-AFTER-CAPACITY-GATE');
  else requireRoute(request.firstCutoverConfirmation === '');
  let action;
  if (resolved.model === 'integrated') {
    requireRoute(!request.firstCutover && !request.returnRequested);
    if (bindingCurrent !== true) {
      requireRoute(bindingCurrent === false && request.rollbackMode === 'none');
      action = 'integrated-historical-maintenance';
    } else if (request.rollbackMode === 'none') action = 'integrated';
    else if (request.rollbackMode === 'dry-run') action = 'candidate-dry-run';
    else {
      requireRoute(request.rollbackMode === 'execute' && event === 'workflow_dispatch'
        && request.rollbackConfirmation === 'EXECUTE-CANDIDATE-G-ROLLBACK');
      action = 'candidate-execute';
    }
  } else if (resolved.model === 'candidate-g') {
    if (resolved.initialCutoverRequired === true) {
      requireRoute(request.rollbackMode === 'none' && !request.returnRequested
        && Number.isSafeInteger(Number(resolved.centralVersion)) && Number(resolved.centralVersion) > 0
        && /^pages(-recovery)?-[0-9]+-[0-9]+$/.test(resolved.deploymentId ?? '')
        && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(resolved.activeImplementationClosureSha256 ?? ''));
      if (request.firstCutover) {
        requireRoute(bindingCurrent === true && resolved.legacySourceRequired === false
          && /^[a-f0-9]{40}$/.test(resolved.sourceHead ?? '')
          && resolved.sourceHead === deadline.identity.GITHUB_SHA);
        action = 'integrated-cutover';
      } else if (bindingCurrent === true) action = 'candidate-maintenance';
      else { requireRoute(bindingCurrent === false); action = 'candidate-historical-maintenance'; }
    } else {
      requireRoute(request.rollbackMode === 'none' && !request.firstCutover);
      if (request.returnRequested) {
        requireRoute(event === 'workflow_dispatch'
          && request.returnConfirmation === 'EXECUTE-INTEGRATED-RAVSCORE-RETURN');
        action = 'integrated-return';
      } else if (bindingCurrent === true) action = 'candidate-maintenance';
      else { requireRoute(bindingCurrent === false); action = 'candidate-historical-maintenance'; }
    }
  } else if (resolved.model === 'legacy-candidate-g') {
    requireRoute(resolved.initialCutoverRequired === true && resolved.legacySourceRequired === true
      && request.rollbackMode === 'none' && !request.returnRequested);
    if (request.firstCutover) {
      requireRoute(/^[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(request.handoffRunId));
      action = 'integrated-cutover';
    } else action = 'candidate-legacy-maintenance';
  } else sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_OPERATIONAL_ACTION_REFUSED');
  if (keys.some(name => process.env[name] !== captured[name])) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_REQUEST_CHANGED');
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  const route = freezeSourceOriginalMetadata({ action, request, resolved, bindingCurrent,
    operationalRow, profileRow });
  // A real GET and the normal full validators issue this private route. It is
  // NOT a producer result, B/S admission, preflight skip, completion or T gate.
  deadline.normalRoute = route;
  return route;
}

// Fixed protected-generation driver. It does not issue a holder, choose a root,
// accept a command list, replace an outcome or declare SOURCE/T complete.
// The first-cutover/no-B and reused-handoff routes remain distinct: this entry
// cannot borrow a baseline owner for either of them.
async function resolveSourceNormalProtectedBootstrap(holder, restored) {
  const issued = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder), owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  const prelude = issued.normalPrelude;
  if (!prelude || !issued.normalRoute || prelude.root !== owner.context.repository
    || sourceOriginalResultOwners.get(restored) !== owner || restored.restored !== true
    || prelude.originalInstall?.result !== restored || prelude.originalInstall.pending
    || prelude.originalPostInstall?.pending !== false || !owner.preparedInstall?.completed
    || owner.activeOperation || prelude.normalProgress?.pending !== false
    || prelude.legacyBootstrap || prelude.waveBootstrap) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_PROTECTED_BOOTSTRAP_NOT_AVAILABLE');
  }
  await assertSourceOriginalProgress(holder);
  await assertSourceNormalProgressBase(holder);
  const bundle = owner.bundles[owner.kind === 'pair' ? 1 : 0];
  if (!bundle || !bundle.manifest || !bundle.rows.size) sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_PROTECTED_BASELINE_REQUIRED');
  // This is the existing normal PRIVATE_RUNTIME_AVAILABLE branch. Its input is
  // the actual verified B/install, never an environment boolean or S alias.
  for (const row of bundle.rows.values()) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
  sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  if (prelude.legacyBootstrap || prelude.waveBootstrap || owner.activeOperation) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_PROTECTED_BOOTSTRAP_REUSED');
  }
  prelude.legacyBootstrap = Object.freeze({ required: false, statelessIntegratedRecovery: false });
  prelude.waveBootstrap = Object.freeze({ required: false, sourceValidated: false });
}

// Error selection only. The actual driver passes its own observed state; this
// helper grants no owner, clock, closure, SAVE, cleanup or release authority.
function retainSourceNormalDriverFailure(driver, state, error) {
  driver.firstFailure ??= state?.childTerminalFailure?.firstFailure
    ?? state?.firstNormalFailure ?? [error];
  driver.caughtFailure ??= [error]; // Retain a later physical/transport error too.
  return driver.firstFailure[0];
}

async function runSourceNormalProtectedGeneration(holder) {
  const issued = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  if (issued.normalDriver || issued.normalRoute || issued.normalPrelude || issued.originalOwner) {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_NORMAL_DRIVER_REUSED');
  }
  const driver = { firstFailure: null, original: null, operation: null, tail: null };
  issued.normalDriver = driver; // retain before the first authentic read or child
  sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'ROUTE');
  try {
    const route = await readSourceOperationalRoute(holder);
    // This fixed entry is the ordinary fresh protected-generation path. It is
    // not the issuer for first-cutover, rollback or reused source handoff.
    if (!['integrated', 'integrated-historical-maintenance'].includes(route.action)
      || route.request.produceHandoff || route.request.handoffRunId) {
      sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_PROTECTED_DRIVER_ROUTE_REQUIRED');
    }
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PRELUDE');
    await beginSourceNormalPrelude(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PREFLIGHT');
    const preflight = await runSourceNormalPreflight(holder);
    if (preflight.shouldRun === false) return preflight; // no B, producer or T claim
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'ADMIN');
    await runSourceNormalAdminPrelude(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'HANDOFF');
    await runSourceNormalHandoffSelection(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'ORIGINAL_SELECTION');
    await runSourceNormalOriginalSelection(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'CHECKPOINT_PROTECTED');
    await runSourceNormalCheckpointRestore(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'ARCHIVED_READERS');
    await prepareSourceNormalArchivedReaders(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PRIVATE_ROOT');
    await prepareSourceNormalOriginalRoot(holder);
    // Cache-file acquisition precedes B installation, while decryption still
    // requires the captured exact B below. These are not interchangeable.
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PROGRESS_CACHE');
    await acquireSourceNormalProgressCipher(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'ORIGINAL_INSTALL');
    driver.original = await runSourceNormalOriginalInstall(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'POST_INSTALL');
    await runSourceNormalOriginalPostInstall(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PROGRESS_BASE');
    await captureSourceNormalProgressBase(holder);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PROGRESS_UNION');
    await runSourceNormalProgress(holder, driver.original);
    sourceDriverDiagnosticStages.set(driver, 'PRODUCER_CHAIN');
    await resolveSourceNormalProtectedBootstrap(holder, driver.original);
    await runSourceNormalDeployedBulkCopy(holder);
    driver.operation = await beginSourceNormalProducerSession(holder);
    await runSourceNormalCurrentHistoryPlan(holder, driver.operation);
    await runSourceNormalGenerationPreparation(holder, driver.operation);
    await runSourceNormalLegacyCurrentRequests(holder, driver.operation);
    await requireSourceNormalQuickProgress(holder, driver.operation);
    await acquireSourceNormalRawCache(holder, driver.operation);
    await runSourceDmiCohort(holder, driver.operation);
    // The existing tail performs raw SAVE, conditional WAM, CP/OM, Weather and
    // always-seal/upload. It owns first-error/semantic-vs-unknown distinctions.
    driver.tail = await runSourceNormalAfterDmi(holder, driver.operation);
    const state = sourceDmiWorkingState(holder, driver.operation);
    await assertSourceOriginalProgress(holder);
    await assertSourceNormalGeneration(holder, driver.operation);
    if (driver.tail !== driver.operation || !state.normalTailSettled
      || state.saveInFlight || state.normalPhase || state.firstFailure !== null) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('NORMAL_PROTECTED_DRIVER_NOT_SETTLED');
    }
    // The actual same operation remains active/retained. No completed=true,
    // owner release, deadline renewal, public handoff or SOURCE/T admission.
    return driver.operation;
  } catch (error) {
    retainSourceNormalDriverFailure(driver, sourceDmiCohorts.get(driver.operation), error);
    rememberSourceDriverFailure(driver, driver.firstFailure[0]);
    const owner = sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(holder)?.originalOwner;
    if (owner && driver.operation) {
      try { await writeSourceNormalFailureDisposition(holder, driver.operation, driver.firstFailure[0]); }
      catch (outcomeError) {
        const operation = sourceDmiCohorts.get(driver.operation);
        if (operation) operation.failureDispositionFailure ??= [outcomeError];
      }
    }
    if (owner) {
      // Unknown stop/close/SAVE still poisons and retains this same owner. Its
      // separate raw failure must not replace an already observed first cause.
      keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, driver.caughtFailure[0]);
      throw driver.firstFailure[0];
    }
    const prelude = sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(holder)?.normalPrelude;
    if (prelude) {
      prelude.firstFailure ??= driver.firstFailure;
      retainedSourcePreludePhases.add(driver);
    }
    throw driver.firstFailure[0];
  }
}

export async function runSourceCompleteEntry() {
  // This is the fixed action's in-process entry, not a holder/clock/root issuer
  // available to an arbitrary CLI. Match the same spelling rule as H: only
  // redundant literal /./ components of the fixed absolute script are allowed.
  const fixedAction = path.join(PRIVATE_RUNTIME_REPOSITORY_ROOT,
    '.github/actions/save-owned-dmi-grib/index.cjs');
  const script = typeof process.argv[1] === 'string'
    ? process.argv[1].split('/').filter(part => part !== '.').join('/') : null;
  if (arguments.length !== 0 || process.platform !== 'linux'
    || process.versions.node.split('.')[0] !== '24' || process.argv.length !== 2
    || script !== fixedAction || process.env.INPUT_OPERATION !== 'source') {
    sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_FIXED_ACTION_ENTRY_REQUIRED');
  }
  // Capture process identity AND transport before the first await. No CLI root,
  // token, duration, JSON receipt or caller factory can issue this private brand.
  const identity = captureSourceDeadlineProcess();
  const metadata = createWeatherGithubMetadataClient();
  const holder = await issuePrivateSourceJobDeadline(identity, metadata);
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  // The normal driver derives route/preflight from actual reads and retains the
  // genuine B/S owner through each child, drain and typed SAVE acknowledgement.
  // The fixed next consumer runs in THIS process before returning. Its local
  // verified T is not hosted publication or a transfer of owner authority.
  const operation = await runSourceNormalProtectedGeneration(holder);
  const issued = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder), prelude = issued.normalPrelude;
  if (operation === prelude?.preflight && operation?.shouldRun === false) {
    // Only the SAME issued prelude and its actual closed normal probe can
    // return the cheap SKIP. A caller boolean/copy is never this identity.
    const state = sourceNormalPreludeState(holder), driver = issued.normalDriver;
    const phase = operation.phase, proof = sourcePreludePhases.get(phase);
    if (state !== prelude || !driver || driver.firstFailure !== null
      || driver.original !== null || driver.operation !== null || driver.tail !== null
      || Object.hasOwn(issued, 'originalOwner') || state.inFlight !== null
      || state.adminCompleted || state.originalInstall
      || state.writers?.some(row => row.closed !== true)
      || !phase || proof?.holder !== holder || proof?.state !== state
      || !state.phases.includes(phase) || phase.name !== 'preflightProbe'
      || phase.completed !== true || phase.transportClosed !== true
      || phase.firstFailure !== null || phase.result?.exitCode !== 0
      || phase.result?.outputs?.should_run !== 'false') {
      sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_PREFLIGHT_SKIP_NOT_OWNED');
    }
    // No B/session/producer/T was issued. No SOURCE-complete output is made.
    return operation;
  }
  await consumeSourceNormalArtifact(holder, operation);
  await stageSourceNormalArtifact(holder, operation);
  await publishSourceNormalArtifact(holder, operation);
  return finalizeSourceNormalOwner(holder, operation);
}


// Private B admission only. No public token, release flag or SOURCE completion.
const sourceOriginalResultOwners = new WeakMap();
const retainedSourceOriginalOwners = new Set();
function sourceOriginalFailure(code) { throw sourceGuardError(`SOURCE_ORIGINAL_${code}`); }
function keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error) {
  if (owner.firstFailure === null) owner.firstFailure = [error];
  owner.phase = 'RETAINED';
  retainedSourceOriginalOwners.add(owner);
  return owner.firstFailure[0];
}
function sourceOriginalOwner(holder) {
  const state = sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(holder);
  if (!state) sourceDeadlineFailure('SOURCE_JOB_DEADLINE_NOT_ISSUED');
  const owner = state.originalOwner;
  if (!owner || owner.holder !== holder || owner.phase === 'RELEASED') sourceOriginalFailure('OWNER_REQUIRED');
  if (owner.firstFailure !== null) throw owner.firstFailure[0];
  assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  const failureWorker = owner.dmiCohort?.failureWorker;
  if (failureWorker && !(failureWorker.cutoff > performance.now())) throw failureWorker.firstFailure[0];
  return owner;
}
// Clock/error retention only; this record grants no owner, stop, SAVE or origin.
function sourceFailureWorkerRecord(previous, jobCutoff, error, caughtAt) {
  return previous ?? Object.freeze({ kind: 'PARENT_FIRST_FAILURE', caughtAt,
    cutoff: Math.min(jobCutoff, caughtAt + 4 * 60_000), firstFailure: Object.freeze([error]) });
}

// Failure-only work consumes the SAME clock, leaving a small part of that
// existing240s for its local forensic disposition. No success/provider budget
// is changed, and neither this remainder nor a file grants stop/SAVE authority.
// Diagnostic projection of an already validated first-failure clock only.
function sourceFailureDispositionClock(clock) {
  return { kind: clock.kind, caughtAt: clock.caughtAt, cutoff: clock.cutoff,
    ...(clock.kind === 'CHILD_FIRST_FAILURE' ? { child: { reason: clock.child.reason,
      caughtNs: clock.child.caughtNs, cutoffNs: clock.child.cutoffNs } } : {}) };
}
function sourceFailureWorkCutoff(state, issued) {
  return state.failureWorker ? Math.min(issued.deadline, state.failureWorker.cutoff - 2000) : issued.deadline;
}
// Physical I/O only, not an issuer, owner or SAVE/restore receipt. Every
// handle is retained before the first awaited operation; failed checks still
// close each provably same owned descriptor once, without replacing raw error.
async function writeSourceFailureDispositionRecord(record, { root, rootPin, contents, checkRoot }) {
  const pack = await import('./lib/private-weather-component-pack.mjs');
  const constants = sourceOriginalFsConstants;
  const retain = (handle, directory = false) => ({ path: directory ? root : record.file,
    handle, fd: handle.fd, directory, stat: directory ? rootPin : null,
    identity: directory ? null : pack.ownFileDescriptorIdentity(handle.fd),
    closeAttempted: false, closed: false,
    journal: { firstFailure: null, closeAttempted: false, closed: false } });
  const close = async row => {
    if (!row || row.closed) return;
    // The existing writer closes on its successful path. A rejected close is
    // never retried; its original handle and journal remain strongly retained.
    if (row.handle.fd === -1) {
      row.closed = row.journal.closed || !row.directory
        && await pack.ownedFileDescriptorClosed(row.fd, row.identity);
      if (!row.closed) sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_CLOSE_UNPROVED');
      return;
    }
    if (row.closeAttempted || row.journal.closeAttempted || row.handle.fd !== row.fd) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_CLOSE_UNPROVED');
    }
    const current = await row.handle.stat({ bigint: true });
    if (row.directory ? !current.isDirectory() || !sameSourceOriginalStat(rootPin, current)
      : !row.identity || !current.isFile() || current.dev !== row.identity.dev || current.ino !== row.identity.ino) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_CLOSE_UNPROVED');
    }
    row.stat = current;
    try { await closeSourceFinalReader(row, row.journal); }
    finally { row.closeAttempted = row.journal.closeAttempted; row.closed = row.journal.closed; }
  };
  try {
    const handle = await fs.open(record.file,
      constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_EXCL | constants.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600);
    record.writer = retain(handle);
    const created = await handle.stat({ bigint: true });
    if (!record.writer.identity || !created.isFile() || created.nlink !== 1n || created.size !== 0n
      || !sameSourceOriginalStat(created, await fs.lstat(record.file, { bigint: true }), true)) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_FILE_CHANGED');
    }
    await checkRoot();
    await writeSourceArtifactDispositionFile(record.writer, contents);
    await checkRoot();
    const reader = await fs.open(record.file, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
    record.reader = retain(reader);
    const opened = await reader.stat({ bigint: true });
    if (!record.reader.identity || !opened.isFile() || opened.nlink !== 1n
      || opened.dev !== created.dev || opened.ino !== created.ino
      || opened.size !== BigInt(Buffer.byteLength(contents))
      || !sameSourceOriginalStat(opened, await fs.lstat(record.file, { bigint: true }), true)) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_FILE_CHANGED');
    }
    await checkRoot();
    const actual = await reader.readFile('utf8');
    if (actual !== contents || !sameSourceOriginalStat(opened, await reader.stat({ bigint: true }), true)) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_READBACK');
    }
    await checkRoot();
    const directory = await fs.open(root, constants.O_RDONLY | constants.O_DIRECTORY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW);
    record.directory = retain(directory, true);
    const directoryStat = await directory.stat({ bigint: true });
    if (!directoryStat.isDirectory() || !sameSourceOriginalStat(rootPin, directoryStat)) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_ROOT_CHANGED');
    }
    await checkRoot();
    await directory.sync();
    await checkRoot();
  } catch (error) { record.firstFailure ??= [error]; }
  finally {
    for (const row of [record.directory, record.reader, record.writer]) {
      try { await close(row); }
      catch (error) {
        (record.closeFailures ??= []).push({ row, error });
        record.firstFailure ??= [error];
      }
    }
  }
  if (record.firstFailure !== null && record.firstFailure !== undefined) throw record.firstFailure[0];
  await checkRoot();
  record.complete = true; // Only own local fsync/readback, never cloud durability.
}

async function writeSourceNormalFailureDisposition(holder, operation, error) {
  // Lookup only. In particular, a poisoned owner must not pass a normal
  // sourceOriginalOwner/admission guard just to record its failure. This tiny
  // private forensic output cannot resume, release, publish or transfer it.
  const issued = sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(holder), owner = issued?.originalOwner;
  const state = sourceDmiCohorts.get(operation);
  if (!issued || !owner || owner.holder !== holder || state?.owner !== owner
    || state.kind !== 'source-normal-producer' || owner.activeOperation !== operation
    || sourceOriginalOperations.get(operation) !== state || state.completed
    || issued.normalDriver?.operation !== operation || issued.normalDriver.firstFailure?.[0] !== error
    || state.failureDispositionWrite) sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_NOT_AVAILABLE');
  state.failureWorker = sourceFailureWorkerRecord(state.failureWorker, issued.deadline, error, performance.now());
  const clock = state.failureWorker, checkClock = () => {
    if (!(performance.now() < clock.cutoff)) sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_EXPIRED');
  };
  checkClock();
  const root = owner.context?.root;
  const rootPin = owner.bundles.flatMap(bundle => [...bundle.rows.values()])
    .flatMap(row => row.directories).find(row => row.path === root)?.stat;
  if (root !== '/tmp/ravradar-private-production-runtime' || !rootPin) {
    sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_ROOT_REQUIRED');
  }
  const checkRoot = async () => {
    const current = await fs.lstat(root, { bigint: true });
    if (!current.isDirectory() || current.isSymbolicLink()
      || !sameSourceOriginalStat(rootPin, current)) sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_ROOT_CHANGED');
    checkClock();
  };
  await checkRoot();
  const cipher = state.savedPhases?.get('cipher');
  const cloudFinalized = cipher?.completed === true && cipher.semanticFailure == null
    && cipher.receipt?.kind === 'WEATHER_CIPHER_V2_FINALIZED'
    && Number.isSafeInteger(cipher.receipt.cacheId) && cipher.receipt.cacheId > 0;
  // Only the existing ordinary phase paths clear these after actual closure.
  // Any poisoned/unsettled owner is conservative STOP_UNPROVEN, never FAILED
  // with a fabricated closed bit. This is observation, not a new stop issuer.
  const stopUnproved = owner.firstFailure !== null || state.firstFailure !== null
    || state.normalPhase != null || state.saveInFlight != null
    || (state.dmiStarted && (!state.outputGeneration || !state.controlCloseObserved));
  const value = {
    schemaVersion: 1, kind: 'SOURCE_PRIVATE_FAILURE_DISPOSITION',
    status: stopUnproved ? 'STOP_UNPROVEN' : cloudFinalized ? 'FAILED' : 'DURABILITY_UNCONFIRMED',
    runId: issued.identity.GITHUB_RUN_ID, runAttempt: issued.identity.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT,
    sourceHead: issued.identity.GITHUB_SHA, targetReferenceAt: state.lockedTarget,
    firstFailure: sourceFailureDiagnostic(error),
    originalBundles: owner.bundles.map(bundle => ({
      bundleContentSha256: bundle.descriptor.bundleContentSha256,
      datasetId: bundle.descriptor.datasetId, productionReferenceAt: bundle.descriptor.productionReferenceAt })),
    localCipherSealed: Boolean(state.sealedCipher && (state.normalPhases ?? []).some(phase =>
      phase.name === 'seal' && phase.completed === true && phase.transportClosed === true
      && phase.firstFailure === null && phase.result?.exitCode === 0 && phase.result.outputs.saved === 'true'
      && phase.outputGeneration?.rows.includes(state.sealedCipher.row)
      && Number(phase.result.outputs.encrypted_bytes) === state.sealedCipher.bytes
      && state.sealedCipher.row.bytes === state.sealedCipher.bytes
      && state.sealedCipher.row.sha256 === state.sealedCipher.sha256)), cloudFinalized,
    physicalStopProven: !stopUnproved, productionAuthority: false,
    sourceComplete: false, saved: false, uploaded: false,
    clock: sourceFailureDispositionClock(clock),
    privatePayloadIncluded: false,
  };
  const contents = JSON.stringify(value) + '\n';
  if (Buffer.byteLength(contents) > 16384) sourceOriginalFailure('FAILURE_DISPOSITION_BOUND');
  const file = path.join(root, 'source-failure-disposition-' + issued.identity.GITHUB_RUN_ID
    + '-' + issued.identity.GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT + '.json');
  const record = { file, value, writer: null, reader: null, directory: null, complete: false };
  state.failureDispositionWrite = record; // Never retry/adopt a partial result.
  try { await writeSourceFailureDispositionRecord(record, { root, rootPin, contents, checkRoot }); }
  catch (error) {
    // Closing a diagnostic must not unpoison the owner or replace its original
    // raw failure. A retained, unproved close is its own explicit poison.
    if (record.closeFailures?.length) {
      state.failureDispositionCloseFailure ??= [record.closeFailures[0].error];
      keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, state.failureDispositionCloseFailure[0]);
    }
    throw error;
  }
  return Object.freeze({ kind: 'SOURCE_PRIVATE_FAILURE_DISPOSITION_LOCAL', status: value.status,
    localSyncedAndReadBack: true, productionAuthority: false, sourceComplete: false, saved: false, uploaded: false });
}

function sourceFailureTightenFrame(clock, cutoff) {
  const frame = Buffer.alloc(49);
  frame.write('RRSCG4F1', 0, 'ascii'); clock.nonce.copy(frame, 8);
  frame[40] = clock.phase; frame.writeBigUInt64BE(cutoff, 41);
  return frame;
}
function requestSourceFailureTighten(state, phase, jobCutoff, error) {
  const worker = state.kind === 'source-normal-producer'
    ? state.failureWorker = sourceFailureWorkerRecord(state.failureWorker, jobCutoff, error, performance.now()) : null;
  if (!phase.control || phase.controlCloseRequested) return;
  phase.controlCloseRequested = true;
  const destroy = () => {
    try { phase.control.destroy(); }
    catch (closeError) { phase.controlCloseFailure ??= [closeError]; }
  };
  try {
    const clock = phase.failureClock;
    if (!worker || !clock) { destroy(); return; } // No issued child clock/child work.
    const cutoff = sourceChildAbsoluteCutoff(Math.min(sourceFailureWorkCutoff(state, { deadline: jobCutoff }), clock.parentCutoff), clock.after, clock.anchor);
    const frame = sourceFailureTightenFrame(clock, cutoff);
    phase.failureTighten = Object.freeze({ worker, cutoff });
    // Half-close only after the fixed failure frame. No ACK or extra reaping.
    phase.control.end(frame, closeError => {
      if (closeError) { phase.controlCloseFailure ??= [closeError]; destroy(); }
    });
  } catch (closeError) { phase.controlCloseFailure ??= [closeError]; destroy(); }
}
function freezeSourceOriginalMetadata(value) {
  if (value && typeof value === 'object') {
    for (const item of Object.values(value)) freezeSourceOriginalMetadata(item);
    Object.freeze(value);
  }
  return value;
}
function sameSourceOriginalStat(left, right, file = false) {
  const fields = file ? ['dev', 'ino', 'mode', 'nlink', 'size', 'mtimeNs', 'ctimeNs']
    : ['dev', 'ino', 'mode'];
  return fields.every(key => left[key] === right[key]);
}
async function checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row) {
  sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder);
  for (const directory of row.directories) {
    const current = await fs.lstat(directory.path, { bigint: true });
    if (!current.isDirectory() || current.isSymbolicLink()
      || !sameSourceOriginalStat(directory.stat, current)) sourceOriginalFailure('DIRECTORY_CHANGED');
  }
  const opened = await row.handle.stat({ bigint: true });
  const current = await fs.lstat(row.path, { bigint: true });
  if (!opened.isFile() || !current.isFile() || current.isSymbolicLink()
    || opened.nlink !== 1n || !sameSourceOriginalStat(row.stat, opened, true)
    || !sameSourceOriginalStat(row.stat, current, true)) sourceOriginalFailure('FILE_CHANGED');
}
async function hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row) {
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
  const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256'), buffer = Buffer.alloc(64 * 1024);
  let position = 0;
  for (;;) {
    sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder);
    const { bytesRead } = await row.handle.read(buffer, 0, buffer.length, position);
    if (bytesRead === 0) break;
    position += bytesRead;
    if (position > row.bytes) sourceOriginalFailure('LENGTH_CHANGED');
    hash.update(buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead));
  }
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
  if (position !== row.bytes || hash.digest('hex') !== row.sha256) sourceOriginalFailure('BYTES_CHANGED');
}
// Private reuse of an actually completed immutable B digest, never a new read.
// Existing owned handles only; no marker, public issuer or added FD lifetime.
const sourceOriginalBundleHashAdmissions = new WeakMap();
const sourceOriginalBundleHashFields = Object.freeze(['dev','ino','mode','nlink','size','mtimeNs','ctimeNs']);
const sourceOriginalBundleDirectoryFields = Object.freeze(['dev','ino','mode']);
function sourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(owner, row) {
  if (process.platform !== 'linux' || sourceJobDeadlineBrands.get(owner.holder)?.originalOwner !== owner
    || owner.phase !== 'READY' || !owner.bundles.some(bundle => [...bundle.rows.values()].includes(row))
    || !Number.isSafeInteger(row.handle.fd) || row.handle.fd < 0
    || !Number.isSafeInteger(row.bytes) || row.bytes < 0 || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(row.sha256 ?? '')
    || sourceOriginalBundleHashFields.some(key => typeof row.stat[key] !== 'bigint')
    || !Array.isArray(row.directories) || row.directories.some(directory =>
      typeof directory.path !== 'string' || sourceOriginalBundleDirectoryFields.some(key => typeof directory.stat[key] !== 'bigint'))) return null;
  return { owner, row, handle: row.handle, fd: row.handle.fd, path: row.path, bytes: row.bytes, sha256: row.sha256,
    stat: row.stat, identity: sourceOriginalBundleHashFields.map(key => row.stat[key]), directories: row.directories,
    directoryRows: row.directories.map(directory => ({ directory, path: directory.path, stat: directory.stat,
      identity: sourceOriginalBundleDirectoryFields.map(key => directory.stat[key]) })) };
}
function sameSourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(left, right) {
  return left !== null && right !== null
    && ['owner','row','handle','fd','path','bytes','sha256','stat','directories'].every(key => left[key] === right[key])
    && left.identity.every((value, index) => value === right.identity[index])
    && left.directoryRows.length === right.directoryRows.length
    && left.directoryRows.every((row, index) => {
      const current = right.directoryRows[index];
      return row.directory === current.directory && row.path === current.path && row.stat === current.stat
        && row.identity.every((value, key) => value === current.identity[key]);
    });
}
async function hashSourceOriginalBundleRow(owner, row) {
  // Same current owner/Git/deadline and exact real FD/named/directory checks.
  // A changed original is refused here, never refreshed into a new admission.
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
  const before = sourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(owner, row);
  const prior = sourceOriginalBundleHashAdmissions.get(row);
  if (prior && sameSourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(prior, before)) {
    await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
    sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder);
    const checked = sourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(owner, row);
    if (sameSourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(prior, checked)) {
      return; // Reused earlier whole digest; deliberately NOT a fresh byte read.
    }
    // An operand can change during awaited checks. Never return a stale hit;
    // fall through to the old full read without refreshing the prior admission.
  }
  await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row); // Whole old read/EOF/hash path.
  sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder);
  const after = sourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(owner, row);
  if (!prior && sameSourceOriginalBundleHashOperands(before, after)) {
    for (const directory of after.directoryRows) { Object.freeze(directory.identity); Object.freeze(directory); }
    Object.freeze(after.identity); Object.freeze(after.directoryRows);
    sourceOriginalBundleHashAdmissions.set(row, Object.freeze(after));
  }
}

async function pinAuthenticatedSourceOriginals(owner, context, selections) {
  if (sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder) !== owner || owner.phase !== 'RESTORING'
    || owner.bundles.length) sourceOriginalFailure('ADMISSION_REUSED');
  retainedSourceOriginalOwners.add(owner);
  owner.context = context;
  for (const selected of selections) {
    const { archiveInventory, verified, descriptor, finalBundle } = selected;
    assertDescriptorMatchesBundle(descriptor, verified);
    const expected = new Map(verified.files.map(file => [`payload/${file.relativePath}`, file]));
    if (!Array.isArray(archiveInventory) || archiveInventory.length !== expected.size + 1
      || archiveInventory.filter(file => file.path === 'manifest.json').length !== 1
      || new Set(archiveInventory.map(file => file.path)).size !== archiveInventory.length) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('INVENTORY_INVALID');
    }
    const bundle = { selected,
      descriptor: freezeSourceOriginalMetadata(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(descriptor))),
      manifest: freezeSourceOriginalMetadata(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(verified.manifest))), rows: new Map() };
    owner.bundles.push(bundle); // retain partial pinning and real handles on any failure
    for (const file of archiveInventory) {
      if (file.path !== 'manifest.json') {
        const declared = expected.get(file.path);
        if (!declared || declared.bytes !== file.bytes || declared.sha256 !== file.sha256) {
          sourceOriginalFailure('INVENTORY_INVALID');
        }
      }
      const destination = path.join(finalBundle, ...file.path.split('/'));
      assertInside(finalBundle, destination, 'SOURCE original');
      const relative = path.relative(context.root, destination);
      if (!relative || relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) || path.isAbsolute(relative)) {
        sourceOriginalFailure('PATH_INVALID');
      }
      const directories = [];
      let directory = context.root;
      for (const component of ['', ...relative.split(path.sep).slice(0, -1)]) {
        if (component) directory = path.join(directory, component);
        const stat = await fs.lstat(directory, { bigint: true });
        if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) sourceOriginalFailure('DIRECTORY_INVALID');
        directories.push({ path: directory, stat });
      }
      const before = await fs.lstat(destination, { bigint: true });
      if (!before.isFile() || before.isSymbolicLink() || before.nlink !== 1n
        || before.size !== BigInt(file.bytes)) sourceOriginalFailure('FILE_INVALID');
      const handle = await fs.open(destination, sourceOriginalFsConstants.O_RDONLY
        | (sourceOriginalFsConstants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0));
      const row = { path: destination, handle, stat: before, directories,
        bytes: file.bytes, sha256: file.sha256 };
      bundle.rows.set(file.path, row); // before the first possible fd/identity failure
      await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
    }
  }
}
async function restoreSourceOriginals(holder, kind, options) {
  const state = assertPrivateSourceJobDeadline(holder);
  if (state.originalOwner || !['single', 'pair'].includes(kind)) sourceOriginalFailure('ADMISSION_REUSED');
  const owner = { holder, kind, phase: 'RESTORING', bundles: [], firstFailure: null, activeReads: new Set(),
    normalBundleReader: sourceNormalArchivedReaders.get(holder) ?? null };
  state.originalOwner = owner;
  try {
    const result = kind === 'single'
      ? await restoreProtectedPrivateProductionRuntimeOwned(options, owner)
      : await restoreExactProtectedPrivateWeatherPairOwned(options, owner);
    if (result.restored ? owner.bundles.length !== (kind === 'pair' ? 2 : 1) : owner.bundles.length !== 0) {
      sourceOriginalFailure('ADMISSION_INCOMPLETE');
    }
    owner.requiresSourceTransform = result.restored && owner.bundles.some(bundle =>
      !same(bundle.descriptor.modelBinding, options.expected.modelBinding)
      || !same(bundle.descriptor.contractHashes, options.expected.contractHashes));
    owner.phase = result.restored ? 'READY' : 'ABSENT';
    sourceOriginalResultOwners.set(result, owner);
    return result;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
async function consumeSourceOriginal(holder, result, bundleIndex, relativePath, consume) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  if (sourceOriginalResultOwners.get(result) !== owner) sourceOriginalFailure('RESULT_IDENTITY_REQUIRED');
  if (owner.phase !== 'READY' || owner.activeReads.size || !Number.isSafeInteger(bundleIndex)
    || typeof consume !== 'function') sourceOriginalFailure('READ_NOT_AVAILABLE');
  const row = owner.bundles[bundleIndex]?.rows.get(relativePath);
  if (!row) sourceOriginalFailure('DECLARED_FILE_REQUIRED');
  const read = { row, pending: 0, eof: false };
  owner.activeReads.add(read);
  try {
    await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
    const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256');
    let position = 0, iteratorClosed = false;
    const chunks = {
      [Symbol.asyncIterator]() { return this; },
      async next() {
        if (iteratorClosed || read.pending) sourceOriginalFailure('READ_OVERLAP_OR_CLOSED');
        if (read.eof) return { done: true };
        read.pending++;
        try {
          sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
          const buffer = Buffer.alloc(64 * 1024);
          const { bytesRead } = await row.handle.read(buffer, 0, buffer.length, position);
          position += bytesRead;
          if (position > row.bytes) sourceOriginalFailure('LENGTH_CHANGED');
          if (bytesRead) {
            hash.update(buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead));
            return { done: false, value: buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead) };
          }
          await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
          if (position !== row.bytes || hash.digest('hex') !== row.sha256) sourceOriginalFailure('BYTES_CHANGED');
          read.eof = true;
          return { done: true };
        } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
        finally { read.pending--; }
      },
      async return() { iteratorClosed = true; return { done: true }; },
    };
    const value = await consume(Object.freeze(chunks));
    if (!read.eof || read.pending) sourceOriginalFailure('READ_INCOMPLETE');
    await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
    owner.activeReads.delete(read);
    return value;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
// INTERNAL normal-operation adapter. A caller must possess the SAME lexical
// A2 holder and actual restore result. Neither paths nor reports issue a scope.
const sourceOriginalOperations = new WeakMap();
async function openSourceOutputRow(owner, root, file, expected = null) {
  const relative = path.relative(root, file);
  if (!relative || relative.startsWith(`..${path.sep}`) || path.isAbsolute(relative)) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_PATH_INVALID');
  const directories = [];
  let directory = root;
  for (const segment of ['', ...relative.split(path.sep).slice(0, -1)]) {
    if (segment) directory = path.join(directory, segment);
    const stat = await fs.lstat(directory, { bigint: true });
    if (!stat.isDirectory() || stat.isSymbolicLink()) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_DIRECTORY_INVALID');
    directories.push({ path: directory, stat });
  }
  const stat = await fs.lstat(file, { bigint: true });
  if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.nlink !== 1n || stat.size > BigInt(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_FILE_INVALID');
  const handle = await fs.open(file, sourceOriginalFsConstants.O_RDONLY | (sourceOriginalFsConstants.O_NOFOLLOW ?? 0));
  const row = { path: file, handle, stat, directories, bytes: Number(stat.size), sha256: null };
  (owner.operationReaders ??= []).push(row); // retain before any further await
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
  const hash = crypto.createHash('sha256'), buffer = Buffer.alloc(64 * 1024);
  let position = 0;
  for (;;) {
    sourceOriginalOwner(owner.holder);
    const { bytesRead } = await handle.read(buffer, 0, buffer.length, position);
    if (!bytesRead) break;
    position += bytesRead; hash.update(buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead));
    if (position > row.bytes) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_LENGTH_CHANGED');
  }
  row.sha256 = hash.digest('hex');
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
  if (position !== row.bytes || expected && (row.bytes !== expected.bytes || row.sha256 !== expected.sha256)) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_BYTES_CHANGED');
  return row;
}
async function compareSourceOriginalOutput(owner, result, index, relative, row) {
  await consumeSourceOriginal(owner.holder, result, index, relative, async chunks => {
    let position = 0;
    for await (const original of chunks) {
      const actual = Buffer.alloc(original.length);
      let offset = 0;
      while (offset < actual.length) {
        const { bytesRead } = await row.handle.read(actual, offset, actual.length - offset, position + offset);
        if (!bytesRead) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_BYTES_CHANGED');
        offset += bytesRead;
      }
      if (!actual.equals(original)) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_BYTES_CHANGED');
      position += actual.length;
    }
    if (position !== row.bytes || (await row.handle.read(Buffer.alloc(1), 0, 1, position)).bytesRead) sourceOriginalFailure('OUTPUT_BYTES_CHANGED');
  });
  await checkSourceOriginalRow(owner, row);
}
async function originalOperationInput(holder, result, sourceRoot, repositoryRoot) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  if (sourceOriginalResultOwners.get(result) !== owner) sourceOriginalFailure('RESULT_IDENTITY_REQUIRED');
  if (owner.phase !== 'READY' || owner.activeReads.size || owner.progressBinding || owner.activeOperation) sourceOriginalFailure('OPERATION_NOT_AVAILABLE');
  const sourceStat = await fs.lstat(path.resolve(sourceRoot));
  const repositoryStat = await fs.lstat(path.resolve(repositoryRoot));
  const source = await fs.realpath(sourceRoot), repository = await fs.realpath(repositoryRoot);
  if (!sourceStat.isDirectory() || sourceStat.isSymbolicLink() || !repositoryStat.isDirectory()
    || repositoryStat.isSymbolicLink() || repository !== owner.context.repository) sourceOriginalFailure('OPERATION_ROOT_CHANGED');
  assertInside(owner.context.root, source, 'SOURCE normal restored output');
  // COMPLETE is the baseline. LATEST remains an immutable no-loss anchor.
  const index = owner.kind === 'pair' ? 1 : 0, bundle = owner.bundles[index];
  if (!bundle) sourceOriginalFailure('BASELINE_REQUIRED');
  const rows = new Map();
  for (const [relative, original] of bundle.rows) {
    if (!relative.startsWith('payload/')) continue;
    const row = await openSourceOutputRow(owner, source, path.join(source, relative.slice(8)), original);
    await compareSourceOriginalOutput(owner, result, index, relative, row);
    rows.set(relative.slice(8), row);
  }
  for (const selected of owner.bundles) for (const row of selected.rows.values()) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
  return { owner, result, index, bundle, source, repository, rows };
}
export async function beginSourceOriginalMigration(holder, result, options) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  try {
    const state = await originalOperationInput(holder, result, options.sourceRoot, options.repositoryRoot);
    if (path.resolve(options.bundleManifestPath) !== state.bundle.rows.get('manifest.json').path
      || options.expectedSourceHead !== state.bundle.descriptor.sourceHead) sourceOriginalFailure('MIGRATION_ORIGINAL_CHANGED');
    const identity = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(options.predecessorDescriptorPath, 'utf8'));
    const expected = protectedPrivateRuntimeSourceIdentity(state.bundle.descriptor,
      PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_POLICY.targetSourceKind, PROTECTED_PRIVATE_RUNTIME_POLICY);
    if (!same(identity, expected)) sourceOriginalFailure('MIGRATION_IDENTITY_CHANGED');
    const output = path.resolve(options.outputRoot);
    assertInside(owner.context.root, output, 'SOURCE migrated output');
    if (output === state.source || await fs.lstat(output).catch(error => { if (error?.code === 'ENOENT') return null; throw error; })) sourceOriginalFailure('MIGRATION_OUTPUT_EXISTS');
    const operation = Object.freeze({});
    sourceOriginalOperations.set(operation, { ...state, kind: 'migration', output, completed: false });
    owner.activeOperation = operation;
    return operation;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
export async function finishSourceOriginalMigration(holder, operation, actual) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder), state = sourceOriginalOperations.get(operation);
  if (!state || state.owner !== owner || owner.activeOperation !== operation || state.kind !== 'migration' || state.completed) sourceOriginalFailure('MIGRATION_NOT_OWNED');
  try {
    const { sourceOriginalMigrationCompletion } = await import('./migrate-post-cutover-private-runtime.mjs');
    actual = sourceOriginalMigrationCompletion(operation, actual);
    // This is invoked lexically by the NORMAL migrator only after its full
    // validators/exact changed-path guard, file writes and actual rename. It
    // accepts no parsed report. The actual returned object is retained here.
    if (actual.source !== state.source || actual.output !== state.output) sourceOriginalFailure('MIGRATION_OUTPUT_CHANGED');
    state.invocationResult = actual;
    for (const row of state.rows.values()) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
    const rows = new Map();
    for (const file of state.bundle.manifest.files) {
      const expected = file.id === 'full-conditions' ? actual.conditionsDigest
        : file.id === PRIVATE_PUBLIC_HOUR_DELIVERY_PACK_FILE.id && actual.publicHourDigest ? actual.publicHourDigest : file;
      rows.set(file.relativePath, await openSourceOutputRow(owner, state.output, path.join(state.output, file.relativePath), expected));
    }
    for (const selected of owner.bundles) for (const row of selected.rows.values()) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
    state.preparedRows = rows; state.completed = true;
    owner.preparedMigration = state; owner.activeOperation = null;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
export async function beginSourceOriginalInstall(holder, result, options) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder);
  try {
    if (options.renameImpl !== undefined || options.removeImpl !== undefined) sourceOriginalFailure('INSTALL_CALLBACK_FORBIDDEN');
    if (sourceOriginalResultOwners.get(result) !== owner) sourceOriginalFailure('RESULT_IDENTITY_REQUIRED');
    let state;
    if (owner.preparedMigration) {
      const migration = owner.preparedMigration;
      if (!migration.completed || owner.activeOperation || owner.progressBinding
        || path.resolve(options.restoredRoot) !== migration.output
        || await fs.realpath(options.repositoryRoot) !== owner.context.repository) sourceOriginalFailure('PREPARED_OUTPUT_REQUIRED');
      for (const row of [...migration.rows.values(), ...migration.preparedRows.values()]) await hashSourceOriginalHandle(owner, row);
      state = { ...migration, source: migration.output, rows: migration.preparedRows };
    } else {
      if (owner.requiresSourceTransform) sourceOriginalFailure('TRANSFORM_CALLER_UNAVAILABLE');
      state = await originalOperationInput(holder, result, options.restoredRoot, options.repositoryRoot);
    }
    const operation = Object.freeze({});
    const repositoryStat = await fs.lstat(state.repository, { bigint: true });
    if (!repositoryStat.isDirectory() || repositoryStat.isSymbolicLink()) sourceOriginalFailure('OPERATION_ROOT_CHANGED');
    sourceOriginalOperations.set(operation, { ...state, repositoryStat, kind: 'install', files: [], packInvocation: null, completed: false });
    owner.activeOperation = operation;
    return operation;
  } catch (error) { throw keepSourceOriginalFailure(owner, error); }
}
export function retainSourceOriginalPackInvocation(holder, operation, invocation, stage) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder), state = sourceOriginalOperations.get(operation);
  if (!state || state.owner !== owner || state.kind !== 'install' || owner.activeOperation !== operation || state.packInvocation) sourceOriginalFailure('INSTALL_NOT_OWNED');
  bindSourceOriginalCpInvocation(holder, operation, invocation, stage);
  state.packInvocation = invocation; state.componentStage = stage;
}
export async function copySourceOriginalInstallFile(holder, operation, source, temporary, destination) {
  const owner = sourceOriginalOwner(holder), state = sourceOriginalOperations.get(operation);
